## SPEC-V3-CONTRACT-CLOSURE

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-07-25T15:10:23.8043554+08:00`
- **Task ID:** `SPEC-V3-CONTRACT-CLOSURE`
- **Skills invoked:** `writing-plans`、`executing-plans`、`git-workflow`、`verification-before-completion`；`finishing-a-development-branch` 仅用于核对收尾约束，因工作直接位于用户指定的 `main` 且没有独立功能分支，不执行合并、PR 或清理步骤。
- **Key prompt/context:** 用户连续提交对 `SPEC_v3.md` 的局部审查意见，要求先判断问题是否存在，再制定并执行只修改 v3 的修复方案；最后要求把本批更新同步到两个上下文文件并提交、推送。修改前本地 `main=6422d10577461c9d145996b2e5146b3dffbfc15f`，`origin/main=f83948112eeadb3a80dc698f018fc7d8b682f656`；执行 `git fetch origin main` 后远端未出现新提交。
- **Specification changes:**
  - 补全 `CanonicalizationV1` 的 Unicode scalar、字符串转义、key 排序和唯一 `CanonicalTimestampV1`，并把兼容性向量扩展到 CTV-01—CTV-07。
  - 冻结唯一 `OPENAI_PUBLIC_API_V1 → https://api.openai.com:443/v1` 映射；profile、Grant、准备请求、逐请求授权记录和适配器目标绑定同一 `endpoint_id`，禁止环境或配置覆盖 base URL，并拒绝跨 origin redirect 重发正文。
  - 将 `PreparedModelRequestV1` 拆为 Mock/OpenAI 封闭变体，冻结各自摘要域、实际 payload byte count 和 `LLMCallResultV1.authorization_record_ref` 的 `ABSENT/PRESENT` 模式约束，避免 Mock 伪造 OpenAI 字段或授权记录。
  - 消除 PREFLIGHT 循环依赖，固定 workspace lease/recovery → Snapshot 前置检查 → 创建并封存唯一 Snapshot → `detect_static` → readiness → BASELINE 的顺序。
  - 用 `RepositoryLocationV1` 和 `DisclosurePathScopeV1` 判别联合表示仓库根、文件与目录；冻结带路径/无路径来源合同，并把 `FinalDiffV1.added_and_replacement_text_bytes` 固定为全部 CREATE/REPLACE 完整 postimage 原始字节之和。
  - 内嵌唯一 `EditablePathPolicyV1`，只允许 `CREATE/REPLACE src/**`；Candidate、FinalDiff、检查、正式验证、批准和持久化全链路复验，保护工件错误优先于一般不可编辑错误，list/read/search 不受可编辑策略限制。
  - 将 `ListFilesEntryV1` 冻结为 `DIRECTORY | TEXT_FILE | NON_TEXT_FILE`，让 List/Read/Search 共享 `SupportedTextFileV1` 分类；非文本文件可进入 Snapshot 和 List，Read 返回 `FILE_NOT_TEXT` 且零正文，Search 稳定累计 `skipped_non_text_count`，并固定 List/Search 排序与继续语义。
- **Post-change review findings closed:** 复审过程中补齐 FinalWriteback policy identity 的 `TREE_INTEGRITY_FAILED`、内建 editable policy 损坏的 `CONFIG_INVALID`、保护工件错误优先级、Search 直接以非文本文件为 root 的零匹配/计数行为，以及 List“目录优先”和总则“按路径排序”的冲突。没有新增 `RunStatus`、`RunPhase`、自定义 endpoint、二进制补丁或第二套 editable policy 来源。
- **Human intervention:** 用户提供外部审查意见并逐项批准局部修复方向；用户未直接编辑本批文件。用户最终明确授权同步 `AGENT_LOG.md`、`TASK_HANDOFF.md`，并执行 commit 与 push。
- **Verification:** 各局部方案均运行针对性全文断言；最终 List 文件类型合同审计的 14 组要求全部为 `True`。`git diff --check -- SPEC_v3.md` 退出码为 0，仅有 Git 的 LF→CRLF 工作树提示；上下文同步前 `git diff --name-only` 只有 `SPEC_v3.md`。本批是文档合同修改，仓库仍无相应实现代码，因此没有把规范中的未来单测描述成已经运行的实现测试。
- **Review boundary:** 本轮进行了逐问题合同审查和修改后机械复核，但没有执行一轮覆盖 `SPEC_v3.md` 全部 571 行差异的独立无背景冷启动实现试验；不得把局部审计描述为完整课程冷启动门槛已经通过。
- **Git evidence:** 本条与 `TASK_HANDOFF.md`、`SPEC_v3.md` 在同一最终提交中记录；为避免提交对象自引用，本条不写自己的最终 commit SHA，Git history 与 `origin/main` 是提交和推送结果的权威证据。
- **Lesson learned:** 局部 Schema 修复必须同时检查邻接合同。修复非文本 List 表示时，如果不复查 Search root 与全局排序，就会留下同一输入在动作校验、结果计数和分页中的不同解释。

## SPEC-V3-SIX-MINIMAL-FIXES

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-07-25T16:05:41.6185785+08:00`
- **Task ID:** `SPEC-V3-SIX-MINIMAL-FIXES`
- **Skills invoked:** `dispatching-parallel-agents`、`git-workflow`、`verification-before-completion`。
- **Key prompt/context:** 用户要求先由两个独立 subagent 审阅 `SPEC_v3.md`：一个复核此前四项问题是否关闭，另一个只寻找新问题；在收到只读报告后，用户批准执行六项最小修复，同时要求控制规格膨胀，最后同步本日志与 `TASK_HANDOFF.md` 并直接提交、推送当前 `main`。
- **Review agents/results:**
  - `/root/verify_four_spec_issues`（McClintock）确认 Mock/OpenAI 请求分离、Snapshot/PREFLIGHT 顺序、editable path policy 和非文本 List 结果四项均已关闭，置信度高。
  - `/root/find_new_spec_issues`（Confucius）独立发现六项新缺口：消息正文与授权来源无覆盖关系、Grant 扣减公式缺失、`candidate_digest` 无摘要域、`adapter_digest` 未定义、真实崩溃产生 `NOT_ATTEMPTED` 不可达、写回后 deadline 终态不唯一。该代理只读，未修改文件。
- **Specification changes:**
  - 新增 `RequestContentSegmentV1`，让正文、类别、路径、摘要和字节数成为单一来源事实；准备请求不再携带独立 `actual_sources`，authorization record 只保留按 message/segment index 精确派生的无正文投影。
  - 冻结 `charge_bytes = OpenAIPreparedModelRequestV1.canonical_byte_count` 及原子累计公式；重复发送重复扣减，并覆盖边界与并发消费。
  - 新增 `CandidateIdentityV1`，规定 `candidate_digest` 只绑定 Snapshot、CandidateTree 和 `FinalDiffV1`；revision ID/父链仅审计。
  - 删除 `FinalWritebackSubjectV1.adapter_digest`；项目 adapter 通过 `validation_manifest_digest` 唯一传递，不再建立第二个身份。
  - 将 Mock `NOT_ATTEMPTED` 限定为可捕获的适配器调用前控制面失败；真实进程崩溃只产生重启停止证据，不恢复 turn 或伪造调用结果。
  - 冻结持久化 deadline：首次写入前过期为零写入 `STOPPED`；任一路径可能已替换后过期时禁止继续写入或自动回滚，进入 `UNRESOLVED/RECOVERY_REQUIRED`，只允许显式 recovery。
  - 没有新增 FR、Run status、Run phase、AC 编号或恢复子系统；§11 关闭清单改为权威章节/AC 索引以抵消新增合同。
- **Human intervention:** 用户先要求第二个 subagent 只整理问题、不得擅自修改；随后批准六项平衡方案并明确授权同步两个上下文文件、commit 和 push。用户未直接编辑本批文件。
- **Verification:** 六项被转化为 11 条只读规格断言并全部为 `True`；旧语义扫描确认 `adapter_digest`、准备请求独立 `actual_sources`、`计数后调用前崩溃` 均无残留。`SPEC_v3.md` 从 176,927 增至 177,053 字节（+0.07%），从 2,088 增至 2,103 行（+0.72%），非空白字符减少 227；`git diff --check -- SPEC_v3.md` 无空白错误，仅有 LF→CRLF 配置提示。
- **Review boundary:** 本轮是两名 subagent 的分工审阅、主代理逐项复核和修改后机械断言，没有执行覆盖完整 v3 的不同 Agent 类型冷启动实现试验，也没有实现代码或运行时测试；不得把本批描述为课程最终 SPEC/PLAN 批准。
- **Git evidence:** 本条、`TASK_HANDOFF.md` 与 `SPEC_v3.md` 计划由同一提交承载；为避免自引用，不在提交前写最终 SHA，提交和 push 结果以 Git history 与 `origin/main` 为准。
- **Lesson learned:** 控制规格膨胀的关键不是少写安全合同，而是让正文、身份和预算各有唯一权威来源，再把 AC、数据模型和关闭清单降为可观察断言与索引。

## T01.1-GATE-FORMAT-MARKDOWN-FINDING-20260804

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-08-04T11:10:00+08:00` (approximate — recorded when the finding was approved and applied).

- **Finding (Critical, plan-level):** The frozen toolchain (ruff 0.16.1, resolved at 1.Ab) includes `"*.md"` in its default file-resolver `include` set, so `ruff format --check --config gates/ruff.toml .` (the exact `GATE_OFFLINE_V1` command forwarded as the `.` repository-root sentinel) analyzes Python code blocks inside normative Markdown documents. `PLAN.md` and `docs/process/superseded-plan-history.md` contain fenced Python code that is not ruff-format-canonical (e.g. the exact 1.Aa test block), so the command exits 1. Reformatting those documents is forbidden (normative, reviewed, byte-referenced content); the closed runner rejects any `--exclude`/config widening argument; and `gates/ruff.toml` could not exclude `"*.md"` without changing its byte-exact contract. No cold-start trial exercised `GATE_OFFLINE_V1` (`-- .`); the `GATE_BOOTSTRAP_OFFLINE_V1` profile uses scoped targets, which is why this surfaced only at Step 15.
- **Precedent:** Same root-cause class as the CS-01 `.venv-gate` finding (frozen ruff scanned a non-Python artifact under `-- .`), which was resolved by a plan revision adding `extend-exclude` and updating the exact test bytes.
- **Human decision (2026-08-04):** The user approved the minimal plan revision (option: "Revise plan: exclude *.md").
- **Applied revision (minimum fix):** `gates/ruff.toml` `extend-exclude` changed from `[".venv-gate"]` to `[".venv-gate", "*.md"]`; `PLAN.md` updated at the 1.Aa gate-configuration display and in the exact 1.Aa test block's expected raw bytes; the working test module updated to the same bytes; evidence re-materialized to rebind `ruff_config_sha256`. No product behavior, runner, scan, lock, or environment change.
- **Second finding of the same class (Critical, plan-level):** after the config revision, the Step 15 gate scan reported `MATCH PLAN.md GENERIC_API_KEY`. PLAN.md line 2948 — the exact RED test block of legacy step 4.E (originally the single literal `"api_key" + "=" + "test-sentinel-value"` written without concatenation) — contained a literal generic-API-key fixture that the frozen raw-byte scan rule matches case-insensitively. This is the CS-01 B1 class (fixture bytes matching the scan), which the plan's own recorded fix resolves by runtime byte concatenation. The literal predates T01.1 and is only scanned because PLAN.md is in the changed set (required by this revision and by Step 21 evidence). Fixed minimally at PLAN.md line 2948: `candidate.write_text("api_key" + "=" + "test-sentinel-value", encoding="utf-8")` — runtime value unchanged (constant-folded to the identical string), preserving the 4.E contract semantics. No other credential-pattern literals exist in PLAN.md (verified with the scanner's quoted/unquoted/URL/PEM semantics).
- **Verification (after the two revisions):** materialize exit 0 (`OK materialize`); 1.Ac integrity `11 passed`; full `GATE_OFFLINE_V1` profile all exit 0: 1.B Target `1 passed`, 1.B Domain `4 passed`, `ruff-format -- .` (6 files already formatted), `ruff-check -- .` (All checks passed), `mypy -- spikes tests/feasibility scripts/bootstrap_gate_env.py` (no issues in 4 source files), `scan_gate_changed_files.ps1` exit 0 (clean), `git diff --check` exit 0. Test precondition additionally asserts the conflicted state exists (clear failure if git ever auto-resolves).

## T01.1-UNMERGED-GIT-FAIL-CLOSED-REPAIR-20260804

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-08-04T16:40:00+08:00` (approximate — recorded at repair verification and commit).
- **Task ID:** `T01.1` conformance repair — gate scan fail-closed on unmerged Git entries.
- **Finding (Important, T01.1 conformance):** The PLAN contract states that an unmerged/ambiguous Git entry is an operational error of the fixed gate scan ("Deleted paths with no working-tree object are omitted; an unmerged/ambiguous Git entry, ... is an operational error"). The committed `scripts/gate_scan.py` did not probe unmerged entries, so in a conflicted worktree `git diff --name-only HEAD` lists the conflicted path and the scan reads its working-tree bytes (conflict markers) — fail-open instead of the declared fail-closed enumeration error.
- **Applied fix (minimum):** `scripts/gate_scan.py` now runs `git ls-files --unmerged` before enumeration and raises the enumeration failure on any unmerged entry, which maps to the closed stable code `GATE_SCAN_GIT_ENUMERATION_FAILED` (exit 2, empty stdout, single stable stderr line). Added `test_gate_scan_fails_closed_on_unmerged_git_entries` to `AbAcIntegrityTests` with a real conflicted-repository fixture (temporary repo, base commit, divergent side/main commits, failing merge) exercising the default hooks; re-bound `gates/evidence/gate-toolchain-v1.json` (`gate_scan_core_sha256` -> `f577f83611bcbf629e77d0d8b131cd6612f5795d611aec8a4bdfccd2fab47399`, `evidence_digest` recomputed to `24766597ee068becea335b50658579ad4869591d58bfad8acefed1af885a9651`).
- **Verification (all observed, exact commands):** `.venv-gate\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_gate_checks.py pytest -- tests/feasibility/gate/test_gate_bootstrap.py -q` -> `12 passed`; `ruff-format -- .` -> 6 files already formatted; `ruff-check -- .` -> All checks passed; `mypy -- spikes tests/feasibility scripts/bootstrap_gate_env.py` -> no issues in 4 source files; `scan_gate_changed_files.ps1` -> exit 0 (clean); `git diff --check` -> clean; evidence digest independently recomputed and byte-equal to the recorded `evidence_digest`.
- **Implementation/Git boundary:** No SPEC/PLAN product change; no new public interface; the WP01 handover decision (PR deferred to WP01 closure) remains in force.
- **Lesson learned:** fail-closed vocabularies need their negative paths probed too — a scan that is correct for every enumerated state can still fail open on a Git state it never considered.

- **Lessons learned:** (1) a "clean worktree" claim must be re-verified by the controller at dispatch time — an implementer can inherit pre-existing files it did not create, and RED evidence must be re-established rather than assumed; (2) Windows named-mutex lifetime semantics: the mutex object dies with its last handle, so abandoned-owner recovery needs a keeper handle — a probe that "always works" in single-process tests can silently create a new mutex instead of recovering the abandoned one; (3) loader integrity is only as strong as its deepest nested digest — every level of a bound evidence record needs its own verification; (4) an exact RED observed by re-running the declared command with the module absent is the only evidence that survives an adversarial review.

## T01.2-P1-REPAIR-20260804

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-08-04T22:10:00+08:00` (approximate — recorded at the repair commit).
- **Task ID:** `T01.2` post-review P1 repair — GO report loader integrity hardening.
- **Findings (P1, external review, controller-confirmed):** (1) `_deserialize_report` trusted the serialized `evaluation` and `outcome` after byte-digest verification only — outcome="GO" with `evaluation.passed=false` was accepted after recomputing the report digest (internal-consistency gap vs GREEN-2 "internally consistent"); (2) the embedded `gate_toolchain` was validated only self-referentially — changing `python_version` to "3.12.999" and recomputing both digests was accepted (root-binding gap vs GREEN-4 "committed terminal file must bind the unchanged Task 1.A evidence byte-for-byte").
- **Applied fix (minimum, no interface change):** shared `_go_predicate` now used by both assembler and loader; loader recomputes `evaluate_workspace_observations` from stored observations and requires exact equality of `passed`/`failed_codes`, then re-derives the outcome via `_go_predicate` and requires equality with the stored outcome; loader reads `root/gates/evidence/gate-toolchain-v1.json`, validates its type/self-digest, and requires the embedded toolchain dict to equal the root file exactly (including `evidence_digest`). Two regression tests added (`test_loader_rejects_internally_inconsistent_evaluation`, `test_loader_rejects_root_toolchain_mismatch`); `_seed_root_toolchain` helper updates all loader fixtures.
- **Verification (all observed):** 25 tests passed (23 + 2 new); controller replayed both attacks independently — tampered evaluation rejected ("evaluation inconsistent with observations"), tampered toolchain with both digests recomputed rejected ("toolchain evidence does not bind root evidence"); untampered round trip loads (GO); SPEC re-review independently re-ran both attacks plus a mutex-field tamper (rejected via shared predicate) and returned `SPEC_REVIEW_PASS` with no residual blocking findings; ruff-format/check clean; strict mypy clean; gate scan exit 0; `git diff --check` clean; `gates/evidence/workspace-boundary-go-v1.json` byte-unchanged (tracked at `d0ed248`); `load_workspace_boundary_gate_report(Path("."))` against the real root succeeds (GO, python_version 3.12.4, digest `c3bb94c9...`).
- **Implementation/Git boundary:** fix commit contains only `spikes/win32_workspace_boundary/report.py`, `tests/feasibility/windows/test_workspace_boundary_gate.py`, and this append-only entry. Branch `codex/wp01`; PR #1 head will be updated on push.
- **Lesson learned:** byte-level digest integrity and semantic self-consistency are orthogonal — a loader that verifies digests but trusts serialized semantics still accepts forged records; every bound evidence level needs both (a) its own digest and (b) re-derivation from the facts it claims to represent. Root files must be the authority: embedded records should be compared to them, not just to themselves.

## T01.2-P1-COERCION-REPAIR-20260804

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-08-04T23:10:00+08:00` (approximate — recorded at the repair; working-tree changes, commit pending).
- **Task ID:** `T01.2` post-review P1 repair (round 2) — loader strict JSON type validation.
- **Finding (P1, external review, controller-confirmed):** `_deserialize_report` parsed every evidence field through implicit coercion — `bool(...)` / `int(...)` / `str(...)` with `get(key, default)` fallbacks. `bool("false")` is `True` in Python, so an evidence record with `object_probe.cleanup_verified` set to the string `"false"` and the evidence digest recomputed loaded as terminal GO (`GO 'false' True`), violating "malformed evidence rejects" and the "cleanup verified before GO" contract. The two prior P1 fixes (evaluation recompute, root toolchain binding) both compared post-coercion values, so neither defense fired.
- **Attack surface (all confirmed by tamper + digest-recompute runs):** bool fields accepting `"false"` / `"true"` / `1` / `null`; int fields accepting `"1"` / `1.5` / `true` — `int(True)` → `1` exactly satisfies `maximum_concurrent_holders == 1`, the hard GO predicate requirement; str fields accepting numbers — `str(4369)` → `"4369"` is valid even-length hex for `bytes.fromhex`, forging file ids; missing fields silently defaulted instead of rejected.
- **Applied fix (minimum, no interface change):** added `_require_bool` / `_require_int` / `_require_str` strict JSON type checkers (int checks exclude `bool` via `isinstance(value, bool)`; missing fields rejected since no default is passed); every field of `gate_toolchain`, `object_probe` observations, `mutex_probe`, and `evaluation` now reads through a strict checker, with `evaluation.failed_codes` validated element-wise; toolchain construction moved ahead of the root-binding compare so type errors are reported as type errors and `True == 1` can never slip past `tc != root_tc`. 26 regression tests: 25 parametrized type-confusion cases (string-bool, numeric-string, float/bool-as-int, `null`, mixed `failed_codes`) plus 1 concrete `"false"`-string bypass test; every case recomputes the toolchain self-digest and the report digest first (full-attacker simulation).
- **Verification (all observed):** baseline `37 passed`; RED before fix `26 failed`; after fix `63 passed` (37 + 26) via `.venv-gate\Scripts\python.exe scripts/run_gate_checks.py pytest -- tests/feasibility`; `ruff-format -- .` → 13 files already formatted; `ruff-check -- .` → All checks passed; `mypy -- spikes tests/feasibility scripts/bootstrap_gate_env.py` → Success: no issues found in 11 source files; `powershell.exe -NoProfile -NonInteractive -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File scripts\scan_gate_changed_files.ps1` → exit 0; `git diff --check` → clean. Attack re-runs: string `"false"` cleanup, string/number/float/bool field types, numeric `failed_codes` entry — all rejected with `ValueError: <field> must be a JSON <type>`; untampered round trip still loads.
- **Implementation/Git boundary:** working-tree changes only — `spikes/win32_workspace_boundary/report.py`, `tests/feasibility/windows/test_workspace_boundary_gate.py`, and this append-only entry. No commit made yet; branch `codex/wp01` at `a52e86f`; worktree otherwise clean. `gates/evidence/workspace-boundary-go-v1.json` byte-unchanged (legitimate evidence unaffected).
- **Lesson learned:** at parse boundaries, implicit coercion folds type errors into valid values BEFORE any recompute-and-compare defense runs, so consistency checks compare post-coercion values and pass — review must build a per-field type contract (field → expected JSON type → coercion on read path) and treat `bool(...)` / `int(...)` / `str(...)` at the boundary as a red flag. Regression suites must tamper with type-confused values (string-bool, numeric-string, bool-as-int, missing), not only type-correct ones, and must recompute every digest like a real attacker. Python-specific traps — `bool("false")` truthiness, `True == 1` equality, `str(4369)` valid hex — are invisible from post-coercion behavior; only the read path reveals them.

## T01.2-P1-UNKNOWN-FIELD-REPAIR-20260804

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-08-04T20:44:09+08:00` (system-observed; this append-only entry follows an earlier approximate `23:10` entry, so textual order is not timestamp order).
- **Task ID:** `T01.2` post-review P1 repair (round 3) — closed-schema unknown-field rejection.
- **Skills invoked:** `test-driven-development`, then `verification-before-completion`.
- **Key prompt/context:** Human explicitly authorized direct repair after external review proved that a digest-recomputed terminal GO containing an extra `object_probe` field still loaded successfully. No subagent was used; the primary agent implemented and verified the repair in the existing WP01 worktree.
- **RED evidence:** Added one five-case nested-path matrix covering report top level, `object_probe`, one observation, `mutex_probe`, and `evaluation`, plus one root-bound `gate_toolchain` case where both embedded and root records contain the same extra field and all applicable digests are recomputed. The exact target run failed `6/6` with `Failed: DID NOT RAISE <class 'ValueError'>`, proving the loader lacked unknown-field rejection.
- **Applied fix (minimum, no serialized-format change):** Added `_require_exact_keys` and fixed field sets for the report, toolchain, object probe, observation, mutex probe, and evaluation objects. The loader now rejects missing and unknown keys at every level; root toolchain evidence is checked against the same closed key set before type/digest validation. Existing strict per-field type validation and digest algorithms are unchanged.
- **GREEN and verification evidence:** Exact target rerun `6 passed`; complete report module `47 passed`; complete feasibility suite `69 passed`. Frozen Ruff format/check passed (`13 files already formatted`, `All checks passed!`), strict Mypy passed (`Success: no issues found in 11 source files`), changed-file credential scan exited 0, and `git diff --check` completed without an error. One initial full-gate run correctly stopped after tests because Ruff requested a one-line test signature; the frozen formatter changed only that signature, after which the target and complete gates were rerun green.
- **Human intervention / Git boundary:** Human authorized the implementation. No commit, push, merge, PR mutation, evidence rewrite, or WP02 work was performed by this repair turn. The existing local coercion-repair commit remains the parent; this repair changes only `report.py`, its feasibility test, and this append-only log entry.
- **Lesson learned:** closed evidence schemas require two independent boundary checks: exact field types and exact field names. Digest validity cannot reject an attacker-controlled extra field when the digest covers that field, and semantic reconstruction cannot reject data it silently ignores; every nested record must therefore enforce its complete key set before interpretation.

## T01.2-P1-SCHEMA-VERSION-QUALITY-REPAIR-20260804

- **Timestamp (Asia/Taipei):** `2026-08-04T21:46:38+08:00` (system-observed).
- **Task ID:** `T01.2` final quality-review repair — reject unknown V1 toolchain schema versions.
- **Quality finding:** Fresh read-only quality review returned `QUALITY_REVIEW_FAIL` with no Critical and one Important finding: strict parsing required `schema_version` to be an integer but did not require the closed V1 value `1`. An attacker could set both root and embedded toolchains to version `2`, recompute the toolchain and report digests, and have the V1 loader accept future/unknown semantics. Two Minor findings concerned an overbroad test description and digest error text that claimed hex validation while checking length before the later equality check.

RED was `Failed: DID NOT RAISE <class 'ValueError'>`. Added shared `_require_schema_version`, used for both root and embedded toolchains, requiring the strict integer value `1`; GREEN was `1 passed`, and the complete report module passed `54 tests`.
- **Minor findings closed:** The missing-field matrix now describes representative fields rather than every field. Digest diagnostics now state the actual early requirement (a 64-character string); digest equality continues to reject non-matching and non-lowercase values without claiming a separate hex-format check.
- **Complete verification (fresh, exact order, all exit 0):** full feasibility suite `76 passed in 2.20s`; Ruff format `13 files already formatted`; Ruff check `All checks passed!`; strict Mypy `Success: no issues found in 11 source files`; changed-file credential scan exit 0; `git diff --check` exit 0.
- **Git / responsibility boundary:** Changes are limited to `report.py`, its feasibility test, and this append-only log. Fixed Task 1.A/toolchain and terminal GO evidence files are not rewritten. Codex primary agent implemented the repair; no subagent edited files. The quality reviewer was read-only; human authorization for commit, push, and final merge review remains active.
- **Lesson learned:** closed-schema validation covers both record shape and schema discriminator values. Exact keys plus exact JSON types still permit an unsupported version unless the discriminator itself is constrained to the one semantic contract the loader implements.

- **Lesson learned:** a frozen recipe file with an un-covered extension silently breaks byte-freeze evidence across checkouts; every file whose bytes are bound by an evidence digest must be covered by the repo EOL rules before commit, and dangling plan citations ("exact §5.1 matrix") must be surfaced rather than silently re-interpreted.

Quality review round 1 `QUALITY_REVIEW_PASS` (0 Critical/Important; 4 Minor: unused `REASON_TOOLCHAIN_SCHEMA`, duplicate `GATE_TOOLCHAIN_EVIDENCE_RELATIVE`, missing `REASON_POLICY_DIGEST` matrix row, cache hygiene) → all four closed → final re-confirmation (fresh read-only reviewer over the complete final diff) returned SPEC PASS and quality PASS with no Critical/Important findings; the reviewer's independent §0.1 digest recomputations matched the frozen manifest digest `896416f1...` and policy digest `b857afca...`.
- **Frozen manifest (`reference/manifest/reference-profile-v1.json`, written exactly once by the passing real run):** canonical compact sorted-key JSON, LF-only, no BOM, no trailing LF (byte-identical to a re-freeze); fields: schema_version 1, profile_id `python-src-py312-v1`, requirements_lock_digest `b3352321...` (== SHA-256 of requirements/reference.lock), docker_image_digest `385ffc69d83536e1874d73517b8b9ee2a0dce6166ca0f30c1f3b1021324ea1a8` (== T02.1 frozen build evidence == registry three-way), docker_execution_profile_version 1, python 3.12.4 / pytest 8.4.2 / report_plugin 1 / ruff 0.16.1 / mypy 2.3.0, check_plan_version 1, editable_path_policy (PYTHON_SRC_ONLY_V1, roots ["src"], operations ["CREATE","REPLACE"], digest `b857afca...`), §0.1 digest `896416f10ed751c4a2ebf763bb3cc6ba0ac90f0ca9e411bdc39c4ca0b93c4bca`.

- **Responsible fresh subagents:** T02.4 implementer (this entry), SPEC reviewer (a9e3016ad69937482, round 1 PASS → re-review PASS), quality reviewer (a5e721dde34b02d50, PASS → Minor closure), final re-confirmation reviewer (a859e60649ce422e0, SPEC+quality PASS). Controller/driver dispatch verified the committed evidence; no file edits by reviewers.
- **Human intervention:** none.
- **Implementation commit:** `55483b6` (8 files, 3437 insertions) via the card's exact `git add` list. Evidence commit: PLAN.md T02.4 Status/checkboxes/Done/Completion evidence + this append-only entry.
- **Lesson learned:** (1) a frozen fixture's TOML can be silently invalid (`strict = True` instead of `true`) and only surfaces when a later task actually runs the toolchain against it — the fail-closed response is a minimal bypass that touches no frozen byte, recorded for the wave owner; (2) `docker cp` never traverses mount points, so a report channel on a tmpfs must be extracted from `docker logs` with a fixed structured prefix — and pytest's own stdout bytes can interleave into the same lines, so extraction must partition on the prefix rather than require line-start alignment; (3) an honest RED for an assembler that "does not check identities" is best re-established by temporarily bypassing the predicate and observing the mismatched input wrongly pass, exactly as the T01.2 missing-field precedent did.

- **Design notes recorded (implementer interpretations, both review stages accepted):** (1) 3.E disposition matrix = any EXTERNAL_CHANGE/UNPROVABLE → UNRESOLVED；all POSTIMAGE → COMMITTED；all {PREIMAGE, ABSENT, POSTIMAGE-on-CREATE-record} → ROLLED_BACK（AC-29 精确 postimage 可恢复 ABSENT）；其余混合（如 POSTIMAGE-REPLACE 邻接未应用路径）→ UNRESOLVED。apply 只执行：COMMITTED 终态记录（逐路径 VERIFIED）、ROLLED_BACK 只删除精确匹配 postimage 的 CREATE 新文件（破坏性点前复验，unlink 后 exists() 防外部重建窗口，OSError 同样 fail-closed）+ 终态记录（逐路径 ROLLED_BACK）；UNRESOLVED/stale digest 零写入零记录变更。REPLACE-at-postimage 的备份恢复未实现——此类状态只能 UNRESOLVED（仅凭字节不可证明恢复，备份读取属后继/生产范围；两阶段评审均判定为卡片 "exact safe preimage/postimage cases only" 范围内、非行为延后）；(2) preview 对缺失/不可读/篡改/工作区不匹配记录 fail-closed 抛 ValueError（driver 指令；T03.1 SPEC 评审的 active-unknown 建议不适用于 preview，已在证据记录）；(3) "exact §5.1 matrix" 悬空引用按 SPEC_PROCESS §49 以卡片 Expected 行为行实施（T02.x/T03.1 先例）；(4) 3.G 身份证据从给定工作区解析（`workspace/gates/evidence/`，§10.3 本地临时仓库约定），由未改动的 Task 1 loader 全量校验；身份缺失/漂移 → NO_GO 占位空 toolchain；EXTERNAL_IDENTITY case 绑定给定工作区的 `src/a.py`（缺失 → 覆盖拒绝先于矩阵）；(5) apply 拒绝终态记录（ValueError）；(6) 跨次运行 report digest 不稳定（case 证据绑定新鲜观察的 Win32 身份），钉死的契约是 outcome + 逐 case pass 稳定性；(7) gate case 证据 digest 绑定 observed dispositions/digests/cleanup 判定，无 reason 字段（固定接口的刻意后果，`_failed_case` 的 reason 仅进 digest）。
- **Environment interruptions (recorded truthfully):** PowerShell 命令分类器两次瞬时不可用（凭证扫描改经 Bash 调用同一 powershell.exe 精确命令完成，exit 0）；无工作丢失，无 API 中断。
- **Responsible fresh subagents:** T03.2 implementer (this entry), SPEC reviewer (fresh read-only, PASS), SPEC re-reviewer (fresh read-only, PASS), quality reviewer (fresh read-only, PASS), quality re-reviewer (fresh read-only, PASS, via coordinator relay). Controller/driver dispatch verifies the committed evidence; no file edits by reviewers.
- **Human intervention:** none.
- **Implementation commit:** `6f2e8a9` (6 files, 3 spike modules + 3 test modules, 2989 insertions) via the card's exact `git add` list. Evidence commit: PLAN.md T03.2 Status/checkboxes/Completion evidence + this append-only entry. Step 33 (finishing/merge) is the WP03 driver's responsibility.
- **Lesson learned:** 真实对象门禁的证据必须绑定实际观察到的 Win32 身份，因此跨次运行的完整证据 digest 天然不稳定——把"确定性"承诺限定在可证明的层面（outcome/逐 case pass），并在测试注释与证据中显式记录，比强行追求不可达的字节级确定性更诚实；评审 Minor 的关闭必须以文件现状为准逐一核验（"已修复"记录本身可能因替换锚点未命中而不真实——本卡 M-6/M-7 即因此被复审拦截，修复后补真实证据）。

- **Lesson learned:** pydantic lax mode coerces float/bool/string into int fields — closed-schema value objects must pin Strict on scalar fields (and the generic value carrier must stay lax because Strict() cannot apply to a bare TypeVar, so strictness belongs to concrete value types); also, dict-literal loop variables reused across matrix sections break mypy strict with joined union types — per-section variable names are the clean fix, and Git Bash must not be used to launch PowerShell scripts with backslash paths.

(2) the immediate-transaction context manager commits on clean exit and rolls back only on an exception, so every failure outcome after the first in-transaction write raises an internal rollback sentinel (`_DecisionRollback`/`_RevocationRollback`/`_AuthorizationRollback`) that rolls the whole transaction back before the closed result returns — a rejected decision/revocation/authorization never persists its reserved idempotency event or any partial write (pinned by `test_revocation_binds_idempotency_event` and `test_failed_authorization_records_no_event`); (3) 15.D replay identity is the already-DECIDED wait row (recorded decision + subject match; the v0001 wait row has no event_id column); REPLAY returns the existing Grant on an approved replay and a different decision on a decided wait is CONFLICT, both with no second Grant and no re-resume; (4) decide() performs the WAITING_USER → RUNNING(AGENT_LOOP) return transition inline in the decision transaction through the LifecycleRules legality gate (`RunRepository.compare_and_transition` opens its own transaction and cannot be nested); REJECT/EXPIRED leave the run WAITING_USER for the loop to terminate ("no resume" pinned); (5) the disclosure service rejects any non-DISCLOSURE_GRANT wait kind at the boundary (BINDING_MISMATCH); (6) 15.F binds the revocation to the exact Grant/Run/subject/event via the T07.3 ledger (scope `disclosure_revoke`, request digest = §0.1 identity of grant/run/subject/revoked_at) and records only successful revocations; exact replay → REPLAY, new event on a revoked grant → ALREADY_REVOKED, event reuse with a different request → REPLAY_CONFLICT, zero mutation of unrelated Grants; (7) 15.E revalidates grant status, subject facts, profile-fact drift, source categories/scopes via the T15.1 matching algorithm, expiry, and the cumulative budget in one immediate transaction; the charge CAS (`consumed_bytes + charge <= budget`) plus the EXHAUSTED-at-equality CASE makes concurrent charges one-winner and never overdraw; an expired ACTIVE grant is settled ACTIVE → EXPIRED in a separate idempotent status-hygiene transaction after the authorization transaction rolled back (the zero-charge failure persists nothing else); (8) the v0004 "unique request identity/charge" registry phrase is interpreted as each record uniquely binding one (request identity, charge) fact pairing with PK authorization_id — no UNIQUE(request_digest) because SPEC re-send semantics re-charge the full canonical_byte_count for an identical request (pinned by the migration test); (9) DisclosureLedger takes the on-disk database path as a construction binding so concurrency tests can open per-thread connections on the same file (sqlite3 connections are thread-bound, T07.2 precedent); (10) T07.2 RunRepository lock/commit and T07.3 IdempotencyRepository are consumed read-only; no T07.x file was modified; (11) `pytest.importorskip("pydantic")` documents the gate-toolchain boundary (T04.2/T05.1 precedent); (12) the `governance/` and `migrations/` packages follow the namespace-package convention.

Controller/driver dispatch verifies the committed evidence; no file edits by reviewers.

- **Lesson learned:** "return a failure result" inside a `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` context manager COMMITS the transaction (clean exit), so an idempotency-event row reserved by a failed revocation/authorization persisted despite the "no mutation" contract — every failure outcome after the first write must raise a rollback sentinel caught outside the transaction, which the reviewer-facing tests (`test_revocation_binds_idempotency_event`, `test_failed_authorization_records_no_event`) now pin. Also: a rollback sentinel reverts ALL in-transaction writes, so a desired status settlement (ACTIVE → EXPIRED) must run in its own idempotent transaction after the rollback rather than inside the failed authorization.

`ReadFileResultV1` etc. are `Annotated[...]` discriminated unions and `FileToolResultV1` is the plain union alias for T17.1; (2) cursor models do NOT self-validate their digest at construction — the TOOL recomputes and compares on every continuation (self-digest -> query digest -> tree digest, via the one shared `validate_cursor_binding`), so tampered cursors are constructible for the tamper matrix and fail closed as `CONTINUATION_INVALID`, while a consistently bound cursor from a drifted tree fails as `CONTINUATION_STALE`; (3) "oversized" (registry 11.A) is the deterministic byte-bound truncation success path (SPEC defines no oversized error; `max_bytes` 1..32768 bounds the body, GREEN-2 "artifact truncation"); "stale identity" is the enumerated-then-drifted read (`read_bytes` KeyError) closing as `FILE_NOT_FOUND`; (5) read body = each requested line with its original terminator (BOM stripped), truncated at `max_bytes` on a Unicode scalar boundary; `end_line` = line containing the last byte of the returned body; `eof = not truncated and end_line == last_line`; `file_digest` = SHA-256 of the complete raw file bytes (BOM included); (6) search matches are per-line non-overlapping literal occurrences; columns are 1-based; case-insensitive matching folds both sides with Unicode casefold and reports the folded match start (documented); excerpts = match line plus up to `context_lines` preceding/following lines joined with the file's terminator, cut to 1024 no-BOM bytes at scalar boundaries; (7) `ListFilesQueryV1.digest` binds `{schema_version, root, recursive, max_entries}` and `SearchTextQueryV1.digest` binds `{schema_version, query, roots, case_sensitive, context_lines, max_results}` (never the cursor — a continuation cannot change its query identity); (8) continuation resumes list at the cursor's exact `(rank, path)` key (>=) and search at `(path, next_match_index)`; zero-match and non-text files always advance the scan position; a cursor pointing past a file's matches or at a non-file position is defensively `CONTINUATION_INVALID` (unreachable with authentic cursors); (9) `ReadableTreeV1` exposes no per-file classification, so List/Search/Read each classify raw bytes exactly once through the shared T10.1 classifier — same bytes always produce the same classification across tools; (10) tool-level error codes `FILE_NOT_FOUND`/`PATH_NOT_DIRECTORY`/`PATH_NOT_FOUND` are the task-owned stable codes for registry rows not named by SPEC (SPEC names `FILE_NOT_TEXT`, `READ_RANGE_OUT_OF_BOUNDS`, `CONTINUATION_INVALID`, `CONTINUATION_STALE`).

- **Lesson learned:** collision closure must be judged against the tree's *normal forms* — exact-path checks pass while casefold/NFC-aliased ancestors (`src/A.py/x` over `src/a.py`, NFD/NFC pairs) still seal file+dir-overlapping trees, so a path-vs-tree collision predicate must fold every proper ancestor prefix and the full path against the existing file/directory sets under exact, casefold, and NFC spellings (the same normal-form discipline the snapshot layer applies at seal time); and a recompute that classifies before the unchanged-skip turns legitimate NON_TEXT snapshot assets into spurious TREE_INTEGRITY_FAILED rejections — the unchanged check must always precede classification.

- **Lesson learned:** a decision memo key must be a function of the DECISION INPUTS, not of identities nothing binds — the semantic digest is an unconstrained 64-hex `StrictStr` in this baseline, so keying the memo on it let a contract-valid spoofed instance turn a hard DENY into a cached ALLOW (the exact bypass T13.1 exists to prevent); the quality reviewer's live probe caught it, and the fix (action type in the key) plus a spoofed-digest regression test pins it; second, process-global `sys.modules` assertions in one task's tests silently break when a later task's modules share the pytest process — the wave-10 merge left exactly this latent interaction (T11.1's assertion vs T12.1's candidate package), which surfaced as the only full-suite failure during this task's closure despite being unrelated to it; third, `Literal[FinalVar]` is rejected by mypy even with `Final[Literal[...]]` — the clean single-spelling pattern is a closed type alias (`FinalWritebackActionTypeV1 = Literal["final_writeback"]`) typed into the `Final` constant.

- **Recorded design interpretations (both review stages accepted):** (1) "exact §5.1 matrix" is the SPEC_PROCESS §49 dangling reference — PLAN registry rows 18.B/18.C/18.D are the operative authorities; (2) an execution root is a UUID-named directory with a sealed `.vespercode-execution-root` marker; WRITABLE_SOURCE = a root not provably allocated by this module, ROOT_REUSE = a root that already holds content, both rejected before any write; (3) link detection combines `os.path.islink` with the Win32 reparse-point attribute bit (`st_file_attributes & 0x400`) because `islink` misses NTFS junctions on Windows (empirically verified; the T09.1 ground truth), and junction removal uses unlink-then-rmdir (rmdir removes the junction itself, the target survives — empirically verified); (4) the bounded collector reads the raw attach socket through the SDK's own `_url`/`_post`/`_get_raw_response_socket` composition with per-read `settimeout`/`recv_into` on the npipe overlapped path, so the deadline is honored even on silent streams, and demuxes the `>BxxxL` multiplexed frames; (5) the attach stream is opened BEFORE `container.start()`, so every byte the container ever produces flows into the collector — the daemon's backlog replay on a late attach is not deterministic on the npipe transport (empirically verified) and is never relied upon; (6) the daemon-side isolation verification runs on the created-but-not-started container, where the frozen daemon reports exactly the one read-only /workspace bind (the /etc/hosts network binds are injected at start); (7) the output cap is the COMBINED stdout+stderr total of one check (the §1.4.5 aggregate reading); (8) `RawExecutionResultV1` carries exact raw bytes; (9) the executor stops/kills the exact container but never removes it — removal is the cleanup contract's; (10) `workspace_unchanged` means the materialized EXECUTION workspace is byte-identical post-run (SPEC §4.5 EXECUTION_WORKSPACE_MUTATED semantics; real-workspace mutation is out of scope), and an already-removed root is vacuously unchanged (idempotent replay); (11) residual artifact priority: surviving materialization root -> surviving container -> drifted workspace (SPEC §4.3 records the exact residual path; failed names are registered non-reusable for the process lifetime); (12) the frozen per-check deadline (120 s, SPEC §4.2.6 ceiling) bounds the container-side phases; (13) the 18.C matrix runs deterministically offline through injectable fake clients (all isolation-drift/deadline/overflow rows), while the real-daemon rows (isolation observations, flood kill, deadline kill, full lifecycle) run in the same marked files; (14) every container is named `vespercode-check-<uuid>` for exact identity and residue observability.
- **Environment interruptions (recorded truthfully):** the safety classifier was temporarily unavailable several times (commands retried; no work lost, no evidence altered); one pytest run hung on the npipe attach stream (transient daemon state; the hang was diagnosed empirically and the attach-before-start redesign removed the race — the flood test now passes reliably); the worktree baseline `b82de8f` predated wave-10 T13.1, so the branch was synced with `origin/main` (`621ffee` merge) before the closure — the stale baseline failed the pre-existing T11.1 import-purity order-dependency test (`src.vespercode.candidate` in sys.modules), which reproduces with the T18.2 files excluded and is fixed on main by `101cef4`; one plan-edit accident (the completion evidence landed on the 7.D card's line because the replacement string was file-wide — detected by verification, 7.D's line restored to "Not yet executed.", the text placed at the T18.2 card's line 6380; 7.D's checkboxes were never touched). - **Implementation commit:** `cfc3984` (10 files, 4404 insertions) via the card's exact `git add` list. Evidence commit: PLAN.md T18.2 Status/32 checkboxes/Completion evidence + this append-only entry. Step 33 (finish WP18-EXECUTION) is the WP18-EXECUTION driver's responsibility.
- **Lesson learned:** real-Docker collection on the Windows npipe transport is full of empirically-discovered traps: the SDK's blocking attach iterator hangs, backlog replay on a late attach is not deterministic, plain `recv` ignores the pipe timeout while the overlapped `recv_into` honors it, `os.path.islink` misses NTFS junctions (only the `st_file_attributes` reparse bit is reliable), `os.walk(followlinks=False)` can descend into junctions, and a recursive rmdir must never be called twice on the same directory; each of these was only pinned by a real-container or real-junction probe, and each became a closed code path with a matrix row. Also: the sealed-evidence schema must bind the EXACT stored representation (raw bytes), never a lossy re-encode; and a file-wide `str.replace` can silently land on the wrong card's line — always target the edit by the surrounding card's unique markers.

- **Implementation commit:** `2c594a4` (9 files, 2935 insertions) via the card's exact `git add` list. Evidence commit: PLAN.md T17.1 Status/32 checkboxes/Completion evidence + this append-only entry. Step 33 (finish WP17) is the WP17 driver's responsibility.
- **Lesson learned:** (1) mypy-strict + verbatim card RED tests can conflict: a RED assertion accessing an error attribute on a union return without narrowing is statically impossible, and the resolution that preserves BOTH the card interface and the verbatim test is a file-level `# mypy: disable-error-code="union-attr"` documented as the minimal accommodation — but the SAME conflict on the production return shape (17.C's `result.error.code`) must be resolved in the type design itself, which is how the T17.1-owned closed optional-error envelope was born; (2) a defensive family check must never itself be able to raise (`_file_success_type` originally used `mapping[action_type]`, and the quality re-review's Important finding was exactly the non-file-port path that made it raise a raw KeyError — a fail-closed guard that raises its own untyped exception is not fail-closed); (3) `json.JSONDecoder().raw_decode` does NOT skip leading whitespace in CPython while `decode` does — the framing contract (whitespace tolerated, trailing content rejected) is exactly `decode`'s behavior; (4) pydantic cannot coerce a JSON string into the `CanonicalRelativePathV1` dataclass, so the parser must perform the one lexical canonicalization before union validation and convert `CanonicalPathErrorV1` into the stable `FIELD_INVALID`; (5) an `Annotated[Union, Field(discriminator)]` TypeAlias has no `model_validate` — validation goes through one shared pydantic `TypeAdapter`; (6) `setattr`-built test spies are invisible to mypy's protocol structural checks — declare the six port attributes as class-level annotations.

- **Lesson learned:** three recurrences this task: (1) an exact RED test's annotation is part of the contract but so is mypy strictness — the T05.1 "minimal mypy-required adjustments" precedent is the right resolution when the displayed annotation forces an `object` index (comment-only, documented); (2) the canonical JSON encoder rejects `None` and `list` — every optional field in a digest body must be an ABSENT/PRESENT wrapper and every array a tuple; and pydantic serializes dataclass fields as `{"value": …}` while the document form is a plain string, so the digest body must be built from the document form explicitly, never from `model_dump()`; (3) "fix the line pairing" is only real when the skip list matches the actual installed module name (`vespercode`, not `src.vespercode`) and the fallback cannot attach stale content to failures that never fired the capture hook — the reviewer's two-round rejection each caught a half-fix; verify a mechanism fix by the environment it runs in (subprocess + container), not by the unit test that passes either way. Plus the T18.2 precedent recurrence: a file-wide `str.replace` landed on the wrong card's line — always bound the edit to the card's region.

full/target reports bind their planned node sets through the T19.1 expectation (a drifted full/target collection is `REPORTER_INVALID`); (2) `run_baseline` owns the execution boundaries: one fresh `allocate_execution_root`+`materialize_candidate` per check over the root candidate of the sealed Snapshot (T12.1), one fresh `ExecutionRequestV1` bound to the plan's frozen manifest/image digests and the closed profile v1 (a drifted plan → `VALIDATION_ENVIRONMENT_CHANGED`), `finalize_execution` per check (workspace drift → `PROJECT_TREE_WRITE` at the runtime check; (4) the runtime compatibility evaluator emits exactly the provable kinds (COLLECTION_UNSTABLE, PROJECT_TREE_WRITE, CHECK_ENVIRONMENT_ERROR); `REPORT_INCOMPLETE` stays closed but unreachable (incomplete reports fail closed at the parse layer first) and `EXTERNAL_SERVICE_REQUIRED`/`VCS_RUNTIME_DEPENDENCY` are constructed-only model values not differentially observable from the closed report schema (their observable class is the session error); (5) `ManifestBindingsV1` carries the two environment digests; `create_validation_manifest` re-verifies them against the frozen profile (`BINDING_MISMATCH`) and sorts the target ids into the manifest's canonical order; the manifest model re-validates every closed invariant (sorted orders, records covering exactly the collection, PRESENT fingerprint exactly on target CALL/FAIL records, digest self-binding) so no drifted Manifest can exist; (6) the fingerprint normalization allowlist is the in-container spelling (`/workspace`, `/tmp`) plus the per-check request/container ids — container output can never reference the host materialization root; (7) the docker-test workspace is the statically-supported normal form (T20.1 matrix shape) with the pytest-8 `pythonpath = ["src"]` ini option, because the frozen fixture's pyproject.toml is invalid TOML (T02.1 evidence byte) and its `import vesper_fixture` cannot resolve under profile v1's frozen environment (T02.4's PYTHONPATH/`-c /dev/null` probe workaround is unavailable in profile v1); (8) the "exact §5.1 matrix" is the SPEC_PROCESS §49 dangling reference — the registry 20.B row (PLAN.md:11402) is the operative authority.

- **Lesson learned:** (1) the predicate layer must be built against the PRODUCTION report shape, not the test's own scripted shape — the quality reviewer's real-container capture (one TEST_PHASE event per phase, 10 events for a 2-test run) exposed that the exactly-one-event predicate made the passing path unreachable with real evidence while the docker test stayed green only because the tool checks block earlier in the loop; scripted evidence is only trustworthy when it mirrors the reporter's emission verbatim; (2) a residue-blocked path must name and preserve its evidence — a `finally: rmtree(ignore_errors=True)` backstop silently destroys the very artifact the cleanup contract leaves as mutation evidence, and a blocked result that cannot name the residual identity is not closed evidence; (3) `Literal[1]` in pydantic lax mode coerces `True`/`1.0` silently — every published identity field needs the explicit `mode="before"` exact-int validator (the T06.1/T05.1 lesson reappeared on a new schema despite the sibling modules all applying it); (4) file-wide checkbox scripts in PLAN.md over-match every card's numbered steps — scope evidence edits by the card's own heading boundaries and verify the diff before committing.

- **Lesson learned:** (1) a "transactional zero-row rejection" contract must also cover encoding failures: pydantic `StrictStr` accepts lone surrogates, and the first UTF-8 encode of user-visible text can raise outside the try block — the quality reviewer caught exactly this; a closed rejection vocabulary is only closed when every input byte sequence maps to a result; (2) validation-before-transaction reads create two failure modes the ledger cannot fix: a later clear makes an exact replay unreachable (scope short-circuit), and a TOCTOU clear between the read and `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` produces a phantom NEW ledger record for a mutation that never happened — the T15.2 `_DecisionRollback` sentinel pattern (ledger first, scope check in-transaction, rollback on rejection) is the established fix; (3) helper/assertion tests that construct commands must vary the unique key per iteration (a loop reusing `entry_id="mem-1"` failed on iteration 2 with a misleading FAILED outcome); (4) ruff reformatting can detach `# type: ignore` comments from the line mypy attributes the error to — re-run mypy after `ruff format`; (5) the T18.2/T19.1 recurring accident class (file-wide edits landing on the wrong region) was avoided this time by asserting the exact card line indices in the one-shot evidence script before writing.

(2) the plan builder recomputes the exact current net diff from the sealed tree bytes (`recompute_final_diff`) and requires the passed `FinalDiffV1.digest` to equal it — a stale diff is `CANDIDATE_STALE`; the passed diff's entries are scanned protected-first then editable-policy (SPEC §4.3 priority) before the recompute; (3) deterministic preflight priority: frozen-profile load → manifest digest self-binding → Snapshot/policy identity chain → environment/reference profile → protected artifact entries → editable policy → exact current diff → protected-artifact set → candidate identity → frozen plan (zero-request atomicity model-enforced); (4) `FormalValidationRequestV1.request_id` is bound to its check kind and ordinal (`formal-<KIND>-<n>`); `FormalValidationBoundsV1` pins the exact SPEC §5.1 300 s/600 s values; per-check deadline enforcement is the T18.1 executor's frozen 120 s bound (stricter, fail-closed) and the phase bound belongs to the run coordinator (GREEN-4 boundary); (5) the coordinator derives `executed_request_ids`/`missing_request_ids`/`duplicate_request_ids`/`complete` from the rows and the evidence model re-validates them as exact closed facts — missing/duplicate/partial execution is explicit and non-success by construction; a drifted plan fails closed at the `ExecutionRequestV1` frozen-built-in rebinding before any container call; (6) the predicate evaluates the SPEC formal success conditions in fixed closed order: binding revalidation (conditions 1/7) → evidence binding/structural completeness → per-request authoritative evidence and teardown/cleanup (conditions 3/4/8) → collection equality (condition 2) → forbidden states and exactly-one-CALL-PASS per node over BOTH pytest rows (conditions 3-5) → tool PASS (condition 6); `VerifiedCandidateV1` is only constructible at the success tail with `formal_result_digest = domain_digest("FormalValidationResultV1", 1, {candidate, manifest, plan, evidence digests})`; (7) the failure vocabulary is the SPEC §4.5 error-list subset applicable to the predicate plus the card-mandated `CANDIDATE_STALE`, with the exact violated condition bound deterministically in `error_message` (SPEC §7.1 respected: no new error codes without a SPEC amendment, which is outside this task's writable boundary — recorded for a future spec revision); (8) the docker-test workspace is the T20.2 supported-normal-form (real fixture calculator/tests bytes + seeded report plugin + `pythonpath = ["src"]`); the reference Manifest's evidence digests are documented synthetic values because the frozen image cannot produce real target-rerun/Ruff/Mypy evidence (T20.2 evidence) — the formal plan/execution consume only the identity fields; (9) the "exact §5.1 matrix" is the SPEC_PROCESS §49 dangling reference — the card Expected lines are the operative matrix authorities.

Docker contention: none observed (the wave's other WP window and the full-suite window were avoided).

Controller/driver dispatch verifies the committed evidence; no file edits by reviewers.

- **Lesson learned:** (1) the SPEC §4.5 error list is a closed vocabulary enforced by §7.1 — a task that needs typed sub-failures must either stay inside the listed codes (mapping condition-level failures to `FORMAL_VALIDATION_FAILED` with the violated condition deterministically bound in `error_message`) or amend the SPEC first; granular codes like `TEARDOWN_FAILED`/`COLLECTION_DRIFT` that look natural during design get flagged at SPEC review; (2) a "typed failure" matrix stays meaningful under a collapsed vocabulary by asserting the deterministic message substring alongside the code; (3) the predicate must scan BOTH pytest runs' evidence, not just the full row — a crafted-but-model-valid collect row carrying a session error is a real evasion path; (4) `model_construct` probes for digest two-pass construction must be fed the nested model INSTANCES, not `model_dump()` dicts (the digest body functions access model attributes); (5) pydantic `model_fields` on an instance is deprecated in 2.11 — access it from the class; (6) model_copy tampering for drift fixtures is only honest when the drifted object's digest binds its drifted fields — digest-consistent drifted fixtures preserve each matrix row's specific expected code once self-binding checks are added.

(4) the payload contract: per-event-type key allowlist (10 kinds covering every SPEC §4.7 record kind) plus a global forbidden key set for request/response/body/secret/raw-output classes, ≤8 keys, values 1..512 chars, ≤8 evidence refs of 1..128 chars, secret values rejected; request digests bind the semantic identity (Run, event type, payload facts, evidence refs) excluding the volatile created_at; rejections discovered inside the transaction roll the NEW ledger record back via the T15.2/T22.1 rollback-sentinel pattern; (9) clear_ended_run requires a recorded SUCCEEDED/STOPPED Run; an exact append replay after the clear returns REPLAY with event None — removed content is never resurrected; (10) projection precedence: recorded `RECOVERY_REQUIRED` status or any UNRESOLVED recovery event dominates terminal projection (a recovery-required Run is never shown as PASS/STOPPED; unresolved recovery overrides a lagging terminal run row); a WAITING_USER Run without wait contexts reports `WAIT_CONTEXT_MISSING` instead of fabricating a decision prompt; evidence refs come only from the most recent fact; (11) retention eligibility is fail-closed: strict `created_at < now - 30 days` AND the runs table records SUCCEEDED/STOPPED, while unresolved-recovery-referenced, active, non-ended, ambiguous, and missing-terminal Runs are preserved (a missing run row never proves endedness — the missing-row state is only reachable out-of-band because the FK is always enforced, seeded via a foreign-key-free side connection); deletion runs in deterministic 100-row batches ordered by (run_id, sequence) with idempotent reruns; per-call result counts; (12) `tests/unit/audit/__init__.py` is a recorded plan-level addition (same-basename module collision, pytest prepend import mode).

- **Lesson learned:** (1) a closed-rejection API must cover identity fields too, not only content: pydantic `StrictStr` accepts lone surrogates anywhere, and the domain-digest canonical JSON encode (which runs before the transaction) can raise `CanonicalJsonErrorV1` — the quality reviewer caught both leak paths (payload value encode and digest encode); every input byte sequence, including identity bytes, must map to a closed result; (2) a request-identity digest is only as complete as the semantic fields it binds: omitting `evidence_refs` silently converted a genuinely different request into a REPLAY and dropped the caller's new references — the ledger's "different request is a conflict" contract requires binding every semantic field (T22.1 binds workspace/entry/creator; audit binds run/type/payload/refs), with a dedicated pin test; (3) same-basename test modules across namespace-package directories break pytest's prepend import mode with ImportMismatchError — the repo precedent is an `__init__.py` in the colliding directory (tests/integration/windows/__init__.py), and the fix must be recorded as a plan-level deviation since the card's file list and git add list do not include it; (4) ruff reformatting detaches `# type: ignore` comments from the line mypy attributes the error to (argument lines) — re-run mypy after `ruff format` and put ignores on the exact reported line; (5) a schema index should serve a query that actually exists in the module: the original `(run_id, created_at)` index served no query while retention's global `created_at < ?` scan went unindexed — the quality reviewer's perf note was closed by changing the index before the schema shipped.

(2) 24.A normalizes only typed failures (check FAIL per finding / ERROR / TIMEOUT, action REJECTED/FAILED, control errors; PASS/NOT_RUN checks and SUCCEEDED actions -> empty sequence) into occurrence-unique stable ids binding the source identity and the canonical observation time (`check:{plan}:{index}:{time}`, `action:{sha256(action_id)[:24]}:{time}`, `control:{code}:{time}`), so two occurrences are never confused and exact re-appends replay; the severity ladder is CRITICAL (control) / HIGH (action failures, check ERROR/TIMEOUT) / MEDIUM (check FAIL) / LOW (reserved); `select_feedback` filters unconsumed records (consumed_by_turn is None), orders by (severity rank, newest canonical time, stable id), keeps at most 10 records and 32 KiB of canonical serialization bytes, and anchors the newest required failure (latest time, id tie-break) as the last selected record so neither limit can drop it (the card's own exact RED pins `selection.records[-1].id == "newest-failure"`); byte drops remove the least relevant non-mandatory records from the end of the severity/recency order; (3) 24.B assembles every allowed category from exact typed inputs in the frozen order (SYSTEM HARNESS_PROTOCOL; USER: TASK task/targets/budget, TOOL_RESULT candidate binding (canonical JSON), TOOL_RESULT action results oldest-first, TOOL_RESULT policy facts, FEEDBACK segments of the record canonical JSON (path PRESENT exactly when the record source attribution carries a canonical path), TOOL_RESULT control facts, MEMORY oldest-first, FILE_CONTENT oldest-first, TOOL_RESULT path-bound oldest-first) and trims only the declared optional categories in the SPEC 4.2.4 order (oldest memory summaries, then oldest SUCCEEDED action results, then non-recent FILE_CONTENT/path-bound TOOL_RESULT units) with whole-segment drops and exact byte accounting; FAILED/REJECTED action results and the most recent fragment are never trimmable (spec-literal "oldest successful action summaries" / "non-recent file fragments"); FEEDBACK is never trimmed (Task 24.A already bounds the selection to 10 records / 32 KiB and the SPEC trim list does not include feedback); the projection digest is the one canonical `domain_digest("ContextProjection", 1, {schema_version, messages, source_projection})` over the final trimmed messages and attribution; canonical_byte_count is the exact total segment content UTF-8 bytes (the mode-specific request byte counts are recomputed by T25.2's request builders); when the mandatory content alone exceeds the frozen budget the pure function returns `ContextBudgetFailureV1(error_code="CONTEXT_BUDGET_EXCEEDED", mandatory_byte_count, budget_bytes)` with zero side effects, and a control-plane construction contract violation (surrogate content, non-canonical facts, invalid assembled sources) fails closed with `ContextProjectionErrorV1`; (4) 24.C's v0008 owns exactly Registry row 444: PK `feedback_id`, closed kind/severity CHECKs, canonical created_at, bounded summary/source_ref/bounded_payload/evidence_refs columns, nullable `consumed_by_turn_id` FK -> `agent_turns(turn_id)` — the one-winner unconsumed predicate `consumed_by_turn_id IS NULL` used by the compare-and-consume; append uses the stored row as the replay identity (rows are never deleted, so no T07.3 ledger for append) with whole-sequence atomicity and `_AppendRollback` sentinel on any conflict; `consume_feedback` validates the turn id and the ordered reference identities (duplicates reject before any mutation), then inside one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction records the command in the T07.3 ledger (scope `feedback_consume`, event id = turn_id + 24-hex canonical-refs digest) so the identical command replays stably (REPLAY) while any different command evaluates fresh, checks turn existence, checks every ref exists, and per-ref `UPDATE ... WHERE consumed_by_turn_id IS NULL` with a rowcount check so exactly one turn wins; every rejection raises `_ConsumeRollback` so the whole transaction — the NEW ledger record included — rolls back and missing/duplicate/forged/conflicted refs change nothing; the closed result kinds are CONSUMED/REPLAY/ALREADY_CONSUMED/MISSING_REF/TURN_NOT_FOUND/DUPLICATE_REF/REJECTED/FAILED (FAILED only for real storage exceptions with fixed messages, SPEC 5.4); (5) `ContextProjectionInputsV1.schema_version` defaults to 1 (an internal typed input model, not a wire request parser); (6) the FEEDBACK segment content is the record's canonical serialization (the single authority shared by the 32 KiB selection bound, the projection segment, and the stored row), and `ContextProjectionV1.feedback_refs` derives the ordered ids by parsing it; (7) lone surrogates fail closed at every model boundary (scalar-sequence checks mirroring the T22.1 lesson) with the canonical encoders as the defensive backstop; (8) the 24.B exact-RED `# type: ignore[union-attr]` is a mypy-required minimal deviation from the card body (the card Interface returns the union while the card body accesses a member directly) — documented in the test and accepted by the SPEC reviewer, in the same class as the ruff-wrapped 24.C signature (T17.1 precedent).

Controller/driver dispatch verifies the committed evidence; no file edits by reviewers.

- **Lesson learned:** (1) a "bounded record" contract is only sound when every stored column's DDL bound is enforced by the same model that constructs the record: the SPEC reviewer caught the source-attribution gap (24.A accepted a 217-char canonical path whose 298-char canonical text violated the v0008 256-char column, surfacing as a generic FAILED append) and the quality reviewer caught the evidence-refs JSON-inflation gap (8 x 128 quote chars -> 2073 chars > the 2048-char column) — the fix pattern is a model-level validator computing the exact canonical serialization length so a DDL-incompatible record cannot be constructed and every rejection stays closed; (2) truncation by character index must never emit a dangling surrogate half at the cut — drop a trailing surrogate code unit after the slice (mid-string lone surrogates still fail closed); (3) canonical JSON inflation matters when bounding stored text: the worst model-valid case of a bounded field can exceed the column CHECK under escaping, so the bound unit must be the canonical serialization, not the raw field; (4) when the card Interface returns a union but the card's exact RED body accesses a member directly, a documented line-level type-ignore is the honest tooling deviation (same class as ruff-wrapped signatures per the T17.1 precedent) — both reviewers accepted it once documented; (5) reviewer-found boundary gaps should be closed with exact-arithmetic pins the reviewer can independently recompute (the byte-drop order and trim-survival pins were re-derived from first principles during re-review); (6) ruff reformatting after a review fix requires re-running the full Domain + mypy + format check before re-review dispatch.

- **Lesson learned:** (1) the WP25 call-orchestration card's RED contract (`result.error_code`) forces the orchestrator's own closed envelope even though Task 16.A already owns the SPEC-named type — the T17.1 same-name precedent (dispatcher `ActionResultV1`) is the honest resolution, and the honest NOT_ATTEMPTED extension (ABSENT ref for pre-charge aborts, PRESENT for post-charge stops) is the only truthful representation of the two stop families — both reviewers accepted it once the matrix pinned both; (2) when a command model carries mode-specific facts, the orchestrator's consistency gates are meaningful only if the test can inject drifted FACTS (strings), not drifted requests (the closed pydantic request schemas self-validate) — the drift rows pin each gate before any side effect; (3) reviewer-suggested robustness fixes (exception-type detection instead of message matching, guarded adapter-bind, cross-task invariant asserts at the boundary) should be closed with their own pins so the re-review can verify at file:line; (4) a module-level TypeAlias without a value binding is not importable (`RestartStopReasonV1: Literal[...]` -> ImportError) — the shell must use `TypeAlias = Literal[...]`; (5) the consume-once trace's second identical command returns the T07.3 REPLAY, not ALREADY_CONSUMED (that outcome needs a different turn) — the two outcomes are pinned in two different files deliberately; (6) ruff reformatting after a review fix requires re-running the full Domain + mypy + format check before re-review dispatch.

- **Recorded design interpretations (both review stages accepted):** (1) 「exact §5.1 matrix」 is the SPEC_PROCESS §49 dangling reference — the PLAN §5 registry rows 14.A/14.B/14.C (lines 11380–11382) are the operative matrix authority and each matrix test implements its row; (2) the writeback subject has no endpoint fact — the registry row's 「endpoint」 item is transmitted via `validation_manifest_digest` per SPEC §4.4.2 (the adapter identity), documented in the matrix docstring; (3) a hard DENY never reaches the decision layer (T13.1 blocks it before a wait exists), so the matrix's 「prior DENY」 row is covered structurally: the subject builder rejects non-editable diffs with `PATCH_PATH_NOT_EDITABLE` and the decision vocabulary closes to APPROVE/REJECT; (4) `schema_version` on the value models is the value contract while the v0010 DDL is the storage contract (no schema_version column, mirroring the v0003 disclosure precedent); (5) `subject_digest` is a read-only property equal to the self-digest so the displayed 14.A RED test compiles verbatim (SPEC §4.4.2: digest 作为 subject_digest); (6) the consume EXPIRED outcome comes only from a stored EXPIRED row or `consumed_at > subject.expires_at` with the whole transaction rolled back — no status-hygiene settlement is ever written (Registry row 14.C zero-persistence-calls contract), deliberately diverging from the 15.E ledger's hygiene settlement because the 14.C registry text is explicit; (7) REJECTED approval rows are schema vocabulary — no T14.1 decision path creates one (REJECT creates no approval row), consume treats them as non-consumable, and the matrix inserts one directly; (8) the FinalDiff canonical-entry helper is replicated across the four test files so every Atomic verification command runs each file standalone (a shared helper would couple them); (9) the decision command's carried subject is verified equal to the decision-bound subject digest (fail-closed on contradictory evidence, SPEC §5.2) in addition to the wait-subject and expiry equality checks.
- **Responsible fresh subagents:** T14.1 implementer (this entry), SPEC reviewer (fresh read-only, round 1 PASS with 1 Important + 1 Minor then all closed, re-review PASS), quality reviewer (fresh read-only, round 1 FAIL with 1 Important + 2 Minors then all closed, re-review PASS). Controller/driver dispatch verifies the committed evidence; no file edits by reviewers.
- **Human intervention:** none.
- **PR:** WP14 draft PR is driver-owned (wave 18 finishing, Step 33); the PR URL is recorded at finishing, not by this task subagent.
- **Implementation commit:** `7f0f302` (9 files, 3590 insertions) via the card's exact `git add` list. Evidence commit: PLAN.md T14.1 Status/32 checkboxes/Completion evidence + this append-only entry (Step 33 is untouched and belongs to the WP14 driver).
- **Lesson learned:** (1) a registry-row contract that says 「performs zero persistence calls」 must be honored literally — even a status-hygiene settlement that mirrors an accepted precedent (15.E) is a persistence call and must be dropped or the registry amended; the SPEC reviewer caught the settlement that the matrix had itself codified, so the matrix test must encode the registry text's strongest reading; (2) when a decision command carries both a digest binding (`WaitDecisionV1.subject_digest`) and the payload (`subject`), the digest equality between the two is a separate check from wait-vs-decision equality — contradictory evidence must fail closed (SPEC §5.2) even when the expiry coincidentally matches, and the check must sit before every later gate; (3) the 「already-RED matrix」 tests per the §49 precedent are written by the implementer during the RED phase and turned GREEN by the implementation — the operative authority is the PLAN §5 registry row, and the registry row's fact list (including inapplicable items like 「endpoint」) must be resolved explicitly in the matrix docstring; (4) formatter-forced wrapping of a displayed RED body is a recorded variance, not a compliance failure, when the semantics are identical and the RED evidence (ModuleNotFoundError exit 4) proves the test was genuine — reviewers accepted the explicit record; (5) reviewer-suggested defensive closures (digest cross-checks, non-empty id validators, corruption-branch tests) should each get their own pin so the re-review can verify at file:line; (6) ruff reformatting after a review fix requires re-running the full Domain + full suite + mypy + format check before re-review dispatch.

- **Recorded design interpretations (both review stages accepted):** (1) "exact §5.1 matrix" is the SPEC_PROCESS §49 dangling reference — the PLAN Registry rows 25.A/25.E/25.G are the operative matrix authorities; (2) the stop precedence order is CANCELLED > MODEL_OUTPUT_INVALID_LIMIT(2) > REPEATED_ACTION_LIMIT(3) > NO_PROGRESS_LIMIT(6) > VALIDATE > TURN_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED > CALL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED > WALL_CLOCK_DEADLINE_EXCEEDED (against the smaller applicable Run or wait deadline) > CONTINUE — VALIDATE precedes the budget rows because validation entry consumes no agent turn/call (the completion at the exact turn cap validates), and the deadline row is last because the pre-side-effect deadline enforcement happens at the boundary before each step (a passed deadline never authorizes a new side effect); (3) the budget rows use the POST-count state (the step's own call already consumed), so with max_turns=N the Nth turn completes and then stops — "stops on the exact limit", one-below-limit continues, and no side effect starts after exhaustion (§4.2.6); (4) the ProgressMarker semantics are exactly §4.2.6's (candidate digest change, previously unseen semantic check result, formal-validation entry), the window is bounded at 5 priors (the exact maximum the 6-turn streak needs), and each streak resets only on its declared condition (no-progress on a marker, repetition on a changed (candidate, action, result) key, invalid on a valid output); the repetition key requires the SAME candidate (the "相同候选上" clause); (5) the engine consults the RestartGuard only for states the loop cannot drive (an interrupted ACTIVE turn -> PROCESS_RESTART_DURING_TURN, terminal runs -> RUN_ALREADY_TERMINAL, recovery/persistence -> DEFERRED, coordinator phases -> fail-closed stop); the loop's drive states are RUNNING(AGENT_LOOP) without an ACTIVE turn and WAITING_USER — a RUNNING run between turns is indistinguishable from a restart point, so the 25.F table's PROCESS_RESTARTED_DURING_RUN stop for that state belongs to the run service's restart gate (§4.2.7 "进程重启统一停止"), never to the loop's own entry (the guard itself is untouched and its own tests unchanged); (6) the engine reads the ACTIVE turn via the one-active-turn invariant for close (the T25.1 M2 note "close-on-stop belongs to the caller... the successor reads the turn via the one-active-turn invariant") and never begins or counts a turn — the orchestrator owns the adjacent begin/record_call_started at its post-authorization counting point (T25.2); (7) the T25.2 body-free LLMCallResultV1 (SPEC §4.4.4 keeps response bodies out of SQLite) forces a wiring-supplied ResponseResolverPortV1 — the resolver performs no adapter call, no counting, and no transport; the one eligible call per step is the orchestrator's counted invocation, pinned by the counting/transport evidence in the matrix; (8) wait-requiring aborts go through the LoopWaitProviderPortV1 — the governance wiring (Task 15.D) owns the "does this abort require a declared wait?" decision, the engine only enters/resumes/expires via WaitController; the matrix's wait trace uses the real DISCLOSURE_SCOPE_EXCEEDED abort with real OpenAI children (credential/ledger/transport); (9) the turn-outcome mapping: FAILED for call failures and invalid outputs on BOTH the CONTINUE and stop paths, SUCCEEDED otherwise including DENY (the step completed with feedback); NOT_ATTEMPTED pre-count aborts create no turn; DELIVERY_UNKNOWN is post-count — the turn exists, closes FAILED, v1 never retries and never reconstructs an uncertain response, and the step stops with INTERNAL_ERROR; (10) the engine's stop paths transition the run to STOPPED (best-effort orchestration; the close result IS checked and fails closed); VALIDATE transitions to RUNNING(FORMAL_VALIDATION) — the loop never publishes SUCCEEDED (§4.2.5 step 7); wait entry transitions to WAITING_USER; a FINAL_WRITEBACK approval hands the run to the persistence coordinator via a DEFERRED boundary; (11) the loop evidence binds the dispatched result's semantic digest + payload digest as the semantic result identity and the step context's candidate digest; run-check payloads are published by the successor check-publication wiring, so check digests bind only when the dispatch result carries a PRESENT payload (the pure 25.A evaluator covers the unseen-check marker independently); (12) the boundary counts (turns_executed/calls_executed) are the run's cumulative persisted counts read fresh from the Task 25.B counter rows on every return; (13) the engine's child DI is duck-typed through port Protocols so the recording RED test and the stub children are type-clean while the production children satisfy the protocols structurally.
- **Responsible fresh subagents:** T25.3 implementer (this entry), SPEC reviewer (fresh read-only, round 1 PASS with 5 Minors then same-stage re-review PASS after M1/M2/M3/M4 closures), quality reviewer (fresh read-only, round 1 FAIL with 1 Important F1 + 2 Minors then same-stage re-review PASS after F1/M1/M2 closures). Controller/driver dispatch verifies the committed evidence; no file edits by reviewers.
- **Human intervention:** none.
- **PR:** WP25-LOOP draft PR is driver-owned (wave 19 finishing, Step 33); the PR URL is recorded at finishing, not by this task subagent.
- **Implementation commit:** `6d48f0b` (10 files, 4847 insertions) via the card's exact `git add` list. Evidence commit: PLAN.md T25.3 Status/32 checkboxes/Completion evidence + this append-only entry (Step 33 is untouched and belongs to the WP25-LOOP driver).
- **Lesson learned:** (1) the thin-composition boundary (GREEN-4) is enforceable in review: the engine must be auditable line-by-line for "no copied predicate" — every stop/progress/wait/restart decision must be a child call and `_observe`-style extraction must be limited to digests/flags; (2) a DI surface that must accept recording/stub children needs port Protocols, not concrete classes — the card's "dependency injection of StopEvaluator, TurnBoundary, ..." is duck-typed DI; (3) when a boundary result carries a semantics like "cumulative counts", every return path must honor it — the reviewer caught the WAITING/DEFERRED/restart hardcoded 0/0 returns against the new docstring, and the honest fix was reading the fresh persisted counts; (4) a FINAL_WRITEBACK approval must hand the run to the persistence coordinator, never continue the loop — the phase gate would otherwise misreport a legitimate transition as INTERNAL_ERROR; (5) an invalid-output turn must close FAILED on BOTH the continue and the stop paths — the SPEC reviewer's M1 closure caught the asymmetric close; (6) turn-outcome and close-result handling should be symmetric across every path (VALIDATE/CONTINUE/STOP all fail closed on a non-APPLIED close); (7) the reviewer-discovered engine defects (the CONTINUE close mapping, the writeback handoff) each needed their own regression pin so the same-stage re-review could verify at file:line; (8) ruff reformatting after a review fix requires re-running the full Domain + mypy + format check before re-review dispatch.
